HFT visibility is governed by the authoring policy above.","docket_mode":"quarantine_without_hft","docket_ready":true}},"selected_context_units":[],"support_registry":[{"branch_refs":["root_000266/B001","root_000266/B008"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_109eadd2c1d43b2a57c8","text":"2. **Concealment can be danger and shield.** Evidence: ج ن ن B008, protective cover; ج ن ن B001, covering/concealment; ع و ذ B001/B002 from 114:1, refuge and protective invocation; خ ن س B001 from 114:4, withdrawal into concealment. Structural cue: the same final root-field that names الجنة also contains protective covering. Assigned role: concealment is ambivalent: the enemy hides, but protection also covers. Abductive move: the refuge sought at the beginning asks for a legitimate cover against illegitimate hiddenness. Reading change: “from the jinnah” is not simply a class label; it activates a reversal where the cure uses protective cover against harmful concealment.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000266/B007"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:4","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_31fe37f1aac77b33ee39","text":"4. **Womb/fetus undertone of vulnerable formation.** Evidence: ج ن ن B007, fetus hidden in the womb; ر ح م B003 from 114:0, womb; ع و ذ B003 from 114:1, newly delivered female staying with young; ر ب ب B009, animal just after birth and freshness. Structural cue: the packet begins with mercy/womb branches and ends with concealed-life branches. Assigned role: the final concealed source also recalls vulnerable beginnings and protected formation. Abductive move: the human chest threatened by whisper is like a formative interior whose contents can be guarded or disturbed. Reading change: the final ayah can be read, exploratorily, as extending protection to the earliest/most hidden stages of human inward formation.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_4e27ceaeec260c3c1724","text":"No later ayah exists in this fixed ayah's clipped focus window, so no later-ayah surprise emerged.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000266/B001","root_000266/B005","root_004965/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_5e23f19a71ac7a6b29cd","text":"1. **Two modes of source: concealed and swaying-visible.** Evidence: ج ن ن B005, hidden spirit beings; ج ن ن B001, covering and concealment; ن و س B001, swaying or dangling oscillation. Structural cue: the final ayah pairs الجنة with الناس after “in the breasts of الناس.” Assigned role: الجنة supplies concealed agency; الناس supplies visible/social instability. Abductive move: the pair is not only a taxonomy of beings but a mechanism with hidden and manifest channels. Reading change: the finale reads as locating whisper-sources across a concealed/visible continuum.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000266/B006","root_000266/B010"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_61d0f591cb10bc537b65","text":"3. **An agentive source coexists with an inward-condition image.** Besides hidden beings, ج ن ن `B010` supplies the hidden heart, inner fear, or concealed inner matter, and `B006` supplies the image of a mind veiled by madness. Their adjacency to 114:5's `فِى صُدُورِ` and و س و س `B001` inner talk activates an exploratory second reading. The packet supplies those accepted images; I infer hidden agitation -> impaired discernment as a possible effect-pattern, while retaining `B005` as a distinct agentive reading. `ٱلْجِنَّةِ` can thus evoke both who whispers and what concealed inward disturbance feels like, without forcing a choice between them.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000266/B001","root_000266/B008"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-3","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_7815ea622a7eeecb477f","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"114:6:2\",\"root_000266\",[\"B001\",\"B008\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Gizlenme hem saldırının örtüsü hem de sığınmanın sağlayabileceği koruyucu örtüdür; ayet zararlı saklanma ile koruyucu sakınmayı karşı karşıya getirir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000266/B010","root_000266/B013","root_004965/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_8621c9310d64270de771","text":"3. **Hidden heart and hidden crowd.** Evidence: ج ن ن B010, hidden heart or inner fear; ج ن ن B013, dark mass/body of people; ص د ر B001/B004 from 114:5, chest and source; ن و س B001, swaying people. Structural cue: 114:5 places the whisper in the breasts of people, then 114:6 names جنة and ناس together. Assigned role: الجنة can mark hidden interiority as well as hidden beings, while الناس marks the moving public mass. Abductive move: what is “from jinnah and people” may include both concealed inner agitation and the crowdly human medium that carries it. Reading change: the final pairing blurs inside/outside: threat may arise from unseen agencies, hidden hearts, and human crowds.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000266/B006","root_000266/B010"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-4","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_bb4d66cefb2d8862c834","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"114:6:2\",\"root_000266\",[\"B006\",\"B010\"]]],\"grade\":\"reject\",\"text\":\"Gizli varlık adı, fail sınıfının yanında, kaynağı seçilemeyen iç sıkıntıyı ve ayırt etme gücünün örtülmesini de çağrıştırabilir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000266/B013"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:4","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_e276d0c98cb9f1342879","text":"4. **The anonymous crowd can conceal agency.** ج ن ن `B013` images the dark mass or common crowd of people, and the fixed coordination places that root directly beside `ٱلنَّاسِ`. This lexical overlap makes a hard visible/hidden binary less stable: a human multitude can itself hide the individual source of circulating talk. The packet supplies crowd and people; I infer diffusion through a crowd -> concealment of responsibility. The phrase consequently admits a social reading in which “hidden” describes distributed human agency as well as an unseen class.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000266/B008","root_004965/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_efde954912827dcdd50f","text":"There are no later ayat after 114:6, so the retrospective movement is a full-packet rereading rather than activation by a subsequent verse. The earlier refuge language made ج ن ن B008 unexpectedly important: the same concealment field that names الجنة also supplies the image of protective cover. The repeated الناس activated ن و س B001 across the whole surah, so the final pair became less a flat list of beings and more a two-channel mechanism: concealed hiddenness and visible sway. The basmalah’s ر ح م B003 and the final ج ن ن B007 also created an exploratory womb/fetus undertone, making the ending fold back to the beginning’s mercy-interior.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000266/B007"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-6","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_f763b7d161239cce9fae","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"114:6:2\",\"root_000266\",[\"B007\"]]],\"grade\":\"reject\",\"text\":\"Gizli varlık adı, ceninin rahimde saklı oluşunu da uzaktan çağrıştırarak iç oluşumun en savunmasız evrelerinin korunmasını düşündürebilir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_004965/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_fcaf0b5f27ac0cdfee42","text":"2. **People oscillate between recipient and transmitter.** The fixed `ٱلنَّاسِ` activates ن و س `B001`, swaying or wavering movement. In 114:1-3 people stand under nurture, sovereignty, and devotion; in 114:5 their breasts receive the whisper; here the same noun occupies the source position. The packet supplies that grammatical role reversal; I infer reception -> possible retransmission, not an essential identity of every recipient with a whisperer. The final `وَٱلنَّاسِ` turns the danger inward upon the protected community and makes human agency mobile between roles.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000266/B005"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_fe00f9f0cccce2611595","text":"1. **Concealed and human sources share one delivery mechanism.** ج ن ن `B005` directly supplies the collective of hidden spirit beings, with `B001` sharpening their concealment; `ٱلنَّاسِ` supplies the human source class. The preceding singular relative construction, `ٱلَّذِى يُوَسْوِسُ`, assigns the same whispering function to both coordinated sources. The packet supplies the classes and shared act; I infer source difference does not entail process difference.
